6a3(10): 'das thub pa chen po 'jig rten dbang phyug rnam par dag pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/rig rig pa 'di nyid kyi sku dang /gsung dang /thugs rdo 
6a4(10): rje las phyung ngo //namakha'i dbyis kyi dbus gnas par//laM las skyes pa'i mi g.yo ba//'jigs byed chen po bsam par bya//yi dgas drod kyi steng na 'gying  
6a5(10): zhal bzhi phyag bzhi zhabs kyang bzhi//g.yas pa ral gri rdo rje 'dzin//g.yon na bsdigs mdzub thod khrag go/zhabs bzhi bzhi bdud bzhi'i steng na 'gying //
6a6(10): sna tshogs rus pa'i rgyan ldan zhing //stag gi sham thabs rnam par brgyan//stod du na ra'i lpags pas brgyan//dmar ser gyen du ska 'jigs tshul//ha har 
6a7(10): bzhad cing rta skad tsher//khro bo du ma'i sprin 'phro zhing //zhal rnams gdangs pas mche ba gtsigs//spyan bcu gnyis po rab tu bsgred//'jigs pa yang 
6a8(10): ni 'jigs par byed//char sprin (mi g.yas)me chen gzi//thabs chen 'dod pa 'dis rdzogs byed//de nas bcom ldan 'das thub pa chen po mi 'gyur rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 
6b1(10): 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/rig pa 'di nyid kyi sku dang /gsung dang /thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo //namakha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//yi ge hUM
6b2(10): las byung ba yi//mi g.yo rgyal po gtum po che//bdud rnams skrag byed bsgom par bya//zhal gcig phyag gnyis bsgom par bya 'jigs pa'i sku//pad dkar 
6b3(10): nyi ma'i steng dkyil na//pus mo sa la btsugs nas bzhugs//phyag g.yas ral gri 'dzin pa ste//g.yon na bsdigs mdzub zhags pas brel//spyan gnyis 
6b4(10): zlum la dmar ba ste//gzhon nu khros pa'i cha byad can//rin chen rgyan gyis rnam par brgyan//sbrul dkar po yis do shal byas//stag gi lpags pa'i sham 
6b5(10): thabs can//sna tshogs khro bo rnam mnga' phro//bskal pa'i me yi dbus na gnas//gang gis nyi ma zla ba yis//mdun du rnam par dran byed na//bdud 
6b6(10): rnams ma lus zad nas ni//bde ba chen po lus rnyed 'gyur//a ta sa yi me tog gi/'od ldan bcom ldan 'das lha mched//bdag gi snying ngam snying 
6b7(10): dkyil bsgom///myur du 'dam par dran byed na//bdung rnams ma lus zad nas ni //bde ba chen po lus snyed 'gyur////'dod pa rdzogs byed pa'o//
7a1(10): @#//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//zhal gcig phyag gnyis du ba'i mdog/yang na sngon po bai DUr gzi//bdud rnams brlag par mdzad pa'o//
7a2(10): g.yon brkyang g.yas bskum bzhugs pa 'am//yang na g.yon pa sa la btsugs//'thab tshul rin chen rgyan gyis brgyan//sdig pa thams cad zad byed cing //
7a3(10): blos gros phung po rgyas par byed//de nas thub po chen po bcom ldan 'das thub pa chen po yongs su rgyas pa dpal rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/rig 
7a4(10): pa 'di nyid kyi sku dang /gsung dang /thugs rje las byung ngo //dam tshig sems dpa' lta bu dang //ye shes sems dpa'i snying khar bsgom//'od ni tshe drag po yi  
7a5(10): gzi//shes bya'i sgrib las rnam par rgyal//de yi thugs kar mtshan ma dang //sa bon rtag par dmigs pa ni//las kyi sgrib pa rnam dag rgyu//las kyi las 
7a6(10): rnams bsgrub pa'i phyir//sa bon las ni 'od gzer spro//ye shes (chas)dpa' bkug nas gzhug/de ni mi shes bag chags rnams//blo ldan blo la 'jom mi byed//
7a7(10): de bzhin gshegs pa kun dmigs nas//bdag la dbang bskur mngon dmigs nas//sangs rgyas rnams kyis dbang bskur 'gyur//spyi bor rang gi rigs bsams 
7a8(10): na//rigs kyi bdag pos dbu  brgyan 'gyur//de ltar de la brtson cher bya//'di ni rgyal rnams gnyis 'gyur nas//myur du rgyal ba nyid thob byed//
7b1(10): 'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po las/lha 'byung ba'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o////de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//dag byed ras 
7b2(10): ris bkra shis kyis//sdig pa'i dri ma rnams bsal nas//sgrub po rnams la mchod ster byed//gcang byed bram ze las rnyed dam//rgyal po 'am bu mo bzang 
7b3(10): gi ras /'jam zhing 'thas par brdung bya te//'dod pa bzhin du bri bar bya//rams dang mig sman khu ba dang //sman gsum me tog gnyis ldan pa'i//
7b4(10): khu ba shing sbyin gsum sbyar nas//gzhan yang sna tshogs tshon gyis bri//de la khro bo'i rgyal po mi g.yo ba ni//zhal gcig pa/phyag gnyis pa/g.yas ral 
7b5(10): gri;g.yon sdigs mdzub dang zhags pas brel ba/klus brgyan pa/pad+ma dang /nyi ma dang /tshogs kyi bdag po'i steng na g.yon brkyang bas 'gying zhing /bskal 
7b6(10): pa'i me'i nang du khro bo du ma'i tshogs 'phro ba bri'o//lha'i dbang phyug mi g.yo mgon po ni zhal gsum phyag drug pa/zhal g.yas dkar ba/g.yon dmar ba/zhal re 
7b7(10): re la spyan gsum gsum dang ldan pa/skra ser po gyen du brdzes pa/phyag gsum gsum na ral gri dang /chu gri dang /sti na bsnams pa/g.yon pa gsuM 
7b8(10): na sdigs mdzub dang /'khor lo dang /kha T+wAM ga 'dzin pa/klus brgyan cing thod pa'i do shal can/stag gi sham thabs dang /glang po che'i stod g.yogs /
8a1(10): @#//thod pa lnga'i dbu rgyan can//rus pas brgyan cing /pad+ma dang /nyi ma'i steng na/g.yon brkyang zhing /g.yas bskums pa'i tshul 
8a2(10): gyis 'gying zhing /bskal pa'i me'i gzi mdangs kyi nang na khro bo bcu 'phro ba bri'o//lha min rgyal po mi g.yo ba ni/zhal bzhi re re la yang spyan gsum gsum dang ldan zhing 
8a3(10): phyag bzhi g.yas gnyis na ral gri dang rdo rje/g.yon gnyis na sdigs mdzub dang thod khragstod na mi'i lpags pa gyon zhing stag gi sham thabs/rus pa klus yan  
8a4(10): lag brgyan pa/bskal pa'i me 'bar zhing khro bo'i tshogs sprin du ma 'phro ba/pad+ma dang nyi ma la g.yon brkyang ba'i tshul du gnas shing /skra ser po gyen du brdzes pa bri'o//
8a5(10): bdud thams cad 'dul ba'i mi g.yo ba ni/gtum po zhal gcig phyag gnyis/ral gri dang /zhags pas brel ba/skra nag po 'am ser po gyen du brdzes pa bri'o࿒
8a6(10): rin po che'i cod pan dang /do shal dang /yan lag thams cad brgyan cing /stag gi sham thabs can/sbrul dkar po se ral khar thogs pa/bskal pa'i tshogs kyis 
8a7(10): mnan pa'i khro bo du ma 'khrug cing /pad+ma dang nyi ma la pus mo g.yas pa 'thab stangs kyi tshul du btsugs pa 'am/yang na g.yon ba gshed ma'i tshul du btsugs shing /
8a8(10): lhag ma namakha' la bsgreng zhing bteg pa bri'o//gang yang rang 'dod pa gcig de ltar bris nas/de'i mdun du rang nyid kyi 'dra 'bag tshon sna tshogs kyis bris/ri mo 
8b1(10): mkhan la go ba legs bskyed nas/mdzes pa yon du phul tes bkra shis brjod nas/rang gi gnas kyi dben pa'i phyogs su bzhugs la mngon sum mi g.yo ba bsgrubs te/me tog 
8b2(10): la sogs pas legs par mchod nas/ye shes kyi khro bo yang de nyid la bstim par bya'o//gang yang dngos grub 'dod pas spos dang /me tog dang /sgron me dang /zhal zas 
8b3(10): la sogs pas rgyun du mchod na/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsang sngags 'grub pa la gdon mi za'o//'dir skye bo 'ga' zhig ni mnan pa ste/bskur nas shin tu yang ltung bar 
8b4(10): 'gyur ba'i phyir/sku dben pa'i sa phyogs su sba bar bya'o//zhi rgyas dbang dang bsad pa 'am//ral gri la sogs brgyad rnams dang //mchog gi sangs rgyas thams cad ni|
8b5(10): rgyal ba'i gzugs brnyan las rnyed 'gyur//'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rtog pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po las/bris sku'i le'u ste/gsum pa'o////
8b6(10): de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//gsang ba rnam par dag byed cing //dngos grub myur phyir gsang sngags rnams//rim pa'i dbye ba ngo mtshar nyon//bcu bzhi dbyangs las 
8b7(10): yum gyis brgyan//gsal byed gnyis pa'i dang po la//gsum pa'i lnga pas nye bar brgyan//gsum pa'i gsum pa der bzhag go/lnga pa'i lnga'o brgyad pa la//
8b8(10): dbyangs kyi gnyis pas mdzes par byas//me dang dbyangs bcu gsum ba 'brel//drod gnyis pa dang drag mtha' 'brel/sum cu gsuM dang dbyangs drug 'brel//
11a1(10): @#//mchod rten khru gang nag po'i bum pa'i nang du rang gi srung ba nyid bcug nas mchod rten bang rim gsum na chad stong pa'i nang du tsha tsha lnga bzhag pas/gru gsum kha dgab 
11a2(10): cing par bu lho phyogs su bzhag go/des zla ba gnyis kyis shi nyes so//gal te gzhan gyi rig sngags gcad (par)'dod na/dbang chen gyi dbus su tshogs kyi bdag po bris te/zur thams cad 
11a3(10): rdo rje rgya gram gyis mtshan mar byas nas//dgra thams cad lam gyis mnan nas rdo rje phur pas bsgrub bya'i ling+ga'i steng nan na na/zhag bdun gyis rig sngags chad do//yang na hUM bzhi mgo sprang 
11a4(10): pa'i 'khrul 'khor ro//yang na tsu tsun+d+ra ra'i pa gas pa la 'khrul 'khor rengs pa'i sbyor ba dang bcas pa 'am//spu la sdong bu byas shing tshil la mar me byas nas du ba de sbyar nas//mig la 
11a5(10): byugs nad gras mi mthong ngo//yang na dpral bar thig le bya/des lag pa la bskus na gang reg pa de dbang du 'gyur nges so//rkang pa la btsugs na dbag tshad brgyar 'gro'o//sA lu'i 
11a6(10): phye ma la gzugs su byas nas phye ma nges bskus na dgra rengs par byed do//dur khrod kyi thal ba dang bcas pas dgra bo'i sgo 'am dpung pa la gtor na nad kyis thebs so//tsu tsan+d+ha ra'i pags 
11a7(10): pa la 'khrul 'khor bris te//dbugs kyis brgyangs nas sngags stong bzlas na dgra bo sbos nas mchi'o//de la sngags ni/tad+ya thA/na mo stu ti ma hA ga na pa ti ye swAhA/oM ga ga Na a d+hi pa ta 
11a8(10): ye swAhA/ga Ne shar ya swAhA//ga Na gti bu dzi ta ya swAhA//na mo stu ti ma hA ga napa ti ye swAhA//oM ka ta ka ta/ma Ta ma Ta/da ra dar/bi da ra bi da ra/ha na ha na/gr-i d+na gr-i d+na/d+ha ba d+ha ba 
11b1(10): ga ra ga ra/b+ha nya dza b+ha nya dza/dzaM ba dzaM ba/mo ha mo ha/de hi di ha/d+ha ba ya d+ha ba ya/d+ha n+yaM d+ha n+yaM/sid+d+hi me pra yats+tsha/sa ma ya ma nu sma ra/ma hA ru tra pa tsa na ya swA hA/oM ku ru ku ru swA hA/oM tu ru tu ru swA hA//
11b2(10): oM pu ru pu ru swA hA/oM mu ru mu ru swA hA/oMshan ti pus tiM ku ru swA hA/A ga ta ben du gra bi ta/tsing ta ma ha bi ta ra ga ma gats+tsha ti/ma hA bA ya/ma hA tsa la/ma hA sa ra kra ma/ma hA ha sti/ma hA d+ha kra 
11b3(10): ya;pra tso da ya mi swA hA/oM ku ru ka ru/tsu ru tsu ru/mu ru mu ru/oM ga ga ga ga ga ga ga//oM na mo na maHswA hA/bzlas pa'i sngags la/oM ba ra da a ka swA hA/zhes pa'o//gzhan yang tshogs kyi 
11b4(10): bdag po'i sbyor ba la rlung bcing bar 'dod na/gur gum dang/gi ha~M dang/yung ba ser pos rgyal po'i ras la/dbang chen gyi dbyibs kyi steng du ri rab rdo rje rgya gram gyis mnan pa bris la/yang dang laM 
11b5(10): rim bzhin bkod nas de'i dbus su tshogs kyi bdag po byi ba la zhon pa//pa dog gser btso ma lta bu/phyag bzhi pa g.yas dang po 'grang 'phreng/g.yon dang po rtse gsum/'og ma gnyis kyis 
11b6(10): thod pa bdud rtsis bkang ba 'dzin pa/sna nam mkha' la 'phyar nas lta ba bris te/de'i snying khar zla ba 'am ljang khu'i nang na la yaM laM bris nas/rnal 'byor pas dbang chen yongs su rgyas pa'i bsam gtan 
11b7(10): dang ldan pas/sngags rtsa brgya rtsa brgyad bzlas na rlung bcing ba'i mchog yin no//yang thog gam shing gi rtse mo la gzhag na rlung 'ching bar byed do//da na tshogs kyi bdag po'i /sna tshogs las bsgrub 'di 
11b8(10): bshad bya/rdo rje gsum gyis mtshan pa yi//dbang chen nyis brtseg dbus su ni//zhal gcig phyag bzhi rtse gsum dang//bi d+hi sa la 'greng 'phreng dang//a sho ka ni bzung byas te//byi ba'i steng 
13a1(10): @#//sngon du bsnyen pa song bas dang po kha//gnyis pa snying kha/gsum pa lte ba/bzhi pa phyir bskum pa rnams su dam por gdab bo//spra tshil lam/thal chen nam/bag zan la 
13a2(10): ling ga byas la//skye tshe dang/re'u lcags dang/nya'i rnga gzhog rnams brdungs la khrag dang sbyar nas gro ga la rdo rje rgya gram bris pa'i dkyil 'khor du ming dang sngags spel te bris la//snying khar bzhug na//
13a3(10): oM tsan+d+ra ma hA ro Sha Na a mo ka s+yA staM pa ya kI la ya hU~M phaT ces gdab bo//sgrub bya'i shes sha pa rlung steng du bzhag la dbugs dang bcas par drang ngo//gal te gso bar 'dod na phur pa dang ming bton te/'o ma blug

13a5(10): dmar ba mig gcig yod pa ral gri dang /zhags pa can kha dog ljang nag rtsub cing sems 'khrugs pa/rin po che grag gi phug du gnas pa la dus rtag tu mchod nas/gru gsum thab kyi dbus su 
13a6(10): bsams nas/sha dang khrag dang dug dang/gzugs brnyan gyi zhal du bsregs na myur du mchi'o//rengs par 'dod pas rnga mo'i rus pa la dz+nye ta ya dz+nye ta ya zhes stong rtsa rgyang bsreg go/lkugs par 
13a7(10): 'dod pas ra'i shal b+ha tir zhes khri tsho gsum bsreg go/basmyo bar 'dod pas khyi sha dang/chang dang/dug sna tshogs la un man+ta zhes stong rtsa brgyad bsreg go/dgra bo'i mig long bar 'dod  
13a8(10): pas skyer pa shing dang/bong bu'i rdug pa la at+ta ka ra zhes stong rtsa brgyad bsregs go/sha lnga dang/bdud rtsi lnga dang/rnga mo'i rus pa la ma hA ya zhes stong rtsa brgyad bsres na rmongs par byed do//
13b1(10): yung ba dang /tsan dan la brgya rtsa brgyad drar ban ya dang bcas pa bsregs na yas chad do//sbal pa dzi rang dang/khyi nag po'i sha la stong bsregs tsi tsid tsi te dza la ma phan ya zhes brjod na mdze nang chad do//
13b2(10): khu ba dang/khrag dang/da du ra la su traM tshid zhes stong rtsa brgyad bsregs na mngal skams nges so//gtum po drag po'i nga rgyal gyis/dur ba 'am gi daM dam/lan tshwa 'am/rgya skyegs bsregs na yams 
13b3(10): nad dang/'khrug pa dang 'jig dang 'byung po bltog/zho dang ku mu ta dang sbrang /bya rgod klad pa kha yi tshil/'di rnams bsregs na basmyo nad dang/lgang nad bu mi thon pa sel//kyai gzhan yang

13b7(10): char yang mi phod do////de nas de'i tshe lha'i bdag po dga' rab dbang phyug la sogs pa lha'i bu drug khris//khro bo chen po mi g.yo ba la bstod pa/kyai brtse ba'i sems 
13b8(10): mnga' 'on kyang 'khrug 'dra zhing/dag cing shin tu dag kyang ra ri sna tshogs spras//lha khams kun las rgyal ba'i ral gri 'phyar ba'i tshe//lha min tshogs rnams brgyal bzhing 
7a1(11): @#/ O /ni gnas/tshul 'di rab tu mi shes pas//khyim gyi 'ching ba dam po'i bcings//de nas bcom ldan 'das la gsang ba'i bdag po lag na rdo rjes
7a2(11): mig gnyis 'briI tshugs su bltas nas 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das ngo mtshar to//bde bar gshegs pa ngo mtshar to//bcom ldan 'das 'di ni mthu che  
7a3(11): lags so//bcom ldan 'das khro bo'i rgyal po de'i snying po'i gzi brjid mthu ni bdag gis bzod lags so//bcom ldan 'dangs khro bo'i rgyal po'i snying po 'di ni gdul

7a6(11): na 'dod pa tha dad pa sna tshogs kyi sems can rnams dga' bar byed pa'i gsung gi 'khor lo'i dkyil 'khor 'jig rten gyi khams sna tshogs pa'i bsam pa (tha)dad pa rnams la bstan 
7a7(11): pa de rnams ni 'di'i byin gyi brlabs kyis bdag gis bstan to//gang yang gsang ba'i bdag po byang chub sems dpa' chen po dbang thams cad thob pa'i ye shes kyi stobs (tshul)

8a8(11): lhag ma namakha' la bsgreng zhing bteg pa bri'o//gang yang rang 'dod pa gcig de ltar bris nas/de'i mdun du rang nyid kyi 'dra 'bag tshon sna tshogs kyis bris/ri mo 
8b1(11): mkhan la go ba legs bskyed nas/mdzes pa yon du phul tes bkra shis brjod nas/rang gi gnas kyi dben pa'i phyogs su bzhugs la mngon sum mi g.yo ba bsgrubs te/me tog 
8b2(11): la sogs pas legs par mchod nas/ye shes kyi khro bo yang de nyid la bstim par bya'o//gang yang dngos grub 'dod pas spos dang /me tog dang /sgron me dang /zhal zas 
8b3(11): la sogs pas rgyun du mchod na/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsang sngags 'grub pa la gdon mi za'o//'dir skye bo 'ga' zhig ni mnan pa ste/bskur nas shin tu yang ltung bar 
8b4(11): 'gyur ba'i phyir/sku dben pa'i sa phyogs su sba bar bya'o//zhi rgyas dbang dang bsad pa 'am//ral gri la sogs brgyad rnams dang //mchog gi sangs rgyas thams cad ni|
8b5(11): rgyal ba'i gzugs brnyan las rnyed 'gyur//'phags pa mi g.yo ba'i rtog pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po las/bris sku'i le'u ste/gsum pa'o////
8b6(11): de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//gsang ba rnam par dag byed cing //dngos grub myur phyir gsang sngags rnams//rim pa'i dbye ba ngo mtshar nyon//bcu bzhi dbyangs las 
8b7(11): yum gyis brgyan//gsal byed gnyis pa'i dang po la//gsum pa'i lnga pas nye bar brgyan//gsum pa'i gsum pa der bzhag go/lnga pa'i lnga'o brgyad pa la//
8b8(11): dbyangs kyi gnyis pas mdzes par byas//me dang dbyangs bcu gsum ba 'brel//drod gnyis pa dang drag mtha' 'brel/sum cu gsuM dang dbyangs drug 'brel//
